- **skill-publisher**: Publishes a local skill directory to the API hub catalog and the GCS bundle store. Runs the four-step author pipeline end to end, packing the source tree into a deterministic .skill zip, signing the manifest with an ed25519 private key, uploading the zip to GCS, and registering the signed manifest with API hub as an API plus Version plus Spec triple along with its four attribute values.
